# History: ChoralRoot FM-1, what was done and why

The dated record of the work, moved out of `docs/HANDOFF.md` on 2026-10-11 and kept as it was written (the owner's words
included); the current state is in `docs/HANDOFF.md`. Append-only: a new entry goes at the end, under its day's heading,
and an old entry is never rewritten (a later entry says what changed).

The labels (3b .. 3z, 3y2 .. 3y26, 1 .. 7) are the old hand-over's and other documents cite them. An entry stands under
the day its work was done; one with no date in its text stands under the day it entered the hand-over (by `git log`). An
entry begun on one day and extended later (3b, 3d, 3n, 3q, 3r, 3s) is kept whole under its first day. "The user" and
"the owner" are the same person. The 0.18 entries between 3y16 and 3y17, and "the panel pictures", have no label
and are cited by their titles; 3y17 .. 3y26 are 0.19's, in the order they were committed, and keep the titles in
their headings because other documents cite those too.

## 2026-10-07

2\. **The published release bodies** for v0.1/0.11/0.12/0.13 show the generic text (the workflow lacked a checkout in the
release job; fixed in `39dbe41`). The user can paste `docs/releases/<v>.md` by hand or `gh release edit`.

4\. **M7 finish**: bank by ear (the user), sticker sheet, README/manual (EDITOR.md/VA.md/FM6.md/CZ1.md are most of it),
the Options "Calibrate" entry exists.

5\. **Hardware-only checks nobody has done**: the reset-reason register bits (console `boot`), SAFE MODE on a real crash,
the USB-record clock drift over an hour, Windows enumeration of "ChoralRoot In", the Reddit user's bricked unit (guide
in the Unbricker README).

6\. **Felucca 1.0.5.1 leftovers** not ported: the LED "breath" (fm1_input.h), its UI changes (not used by ChoralRoot).

7\. M8: Orchid captures (secret-chord map, chromatic Key Mode, factory patterns).

3\. **Sounds page: done 2026-10-07** (docs/SOUNDS.md): the installer's Sounds section (`web/fm1sounds.js`) and
`fm1_install.py --sounds / --export-sound / --import-sound / --rename-sound / --delete-sound`, no firmware change,
verified on the device (the backup protocol ran on hardware for the first time). **.syx export / import done the same
day** (SOUNDS.md ".syx export and import": DX7 single voice / bank for FM6, Casio tone dump for CZ-1; the import's
record comes from per-engine templates generated by `tests/sound_templates.c`, pinned by `run_cr_tests.sh`'s `--check`),
verified on the device. Left: more than 32 slots (SOUNDS.md "Not done": not worth it yet).

3b. **The loop indicator: done 2026-10-07** (the user chose B, the corner dial, from
`design/choralroot-fm1-loop-screens.png`; generator `design/make_loop_mockups.py`): `cr_ui.c cu_dial` / `cu_ring` (the
ring only while capturing, in the count-in / undo screens and the L_LOOP / L_SAVE layers; the dial while the loop merely
plays, never in the editor or Options), `cr_draw.c cr_dial` in the top-line strip with its own cache key, the right text
22 px left; checks in `tests/cr_draw_test.c` and `tools/emu/scripts/cr_loop_load.txt` / `test_cr.sh`. Nit left: while an
overdub is armed the top line does not yet say "Dub 3.2" (mock-up 7; `cu_header` builds "Dub" only once the overdub
records). **REC in the dial too: done 2026-10-09** (the user: the ring on REC was "quite invasive", a big circle over
every menu): `cu_ring` (the ring under every screen while capturing) is gone; the ring is drawn only on the count-in and
undo screens (and calibration); `cu_dial_state` (was `cu_loop_plays`) gives the dial's look, `cr_screen_t.dial_mode`
(`CR_DIAL_PLAY / ARMED / REC / OD`) and `dial_dot` (the REC dot, the REC LED's twin via `cu_blink`: lit armed, blinking
recording / overdubbing; hashed with the dial: a blink redraws strip 0 only); `cr_dial` draws the track red while the
first take records and the dot r 3 in the middle; loop sheet states 9a-9c (the designer's dial `rec` / `dot`), 10 the
count-in; fixtures 63-67; `cr_draw_test.c` checks the looks, a lint "on the dial"; `tools/emu/scripts/cr_rec_dial.txt`
(shots `cr_rec_armed / rec / od.ppm`) and test_cr.sh's ring / dial checks on the loop shots. The loop sheet's PNG is not
re-rendered yet.

3c. **Melodee 0.12** (github.com/keremimo/melodee, tag v0.12, fetched into `melodee/`): native user presets (64 FM6
voices, 128 CZ-1 tones as raw tones in the pool, SysEx command 78 per slot, .syx import / export in its editor), 64
general presets (ids 17 / 18), microtonal scales, A4 400-480 Hz, timed recording, note editing; its storage.c leaves the
last 256 bytes of its new objects erased because of the SPL's scan: checked here, the SPL reads only the fixed OTA
record at 0xE4F00 (`ota.c OTA_RES`), so ChoralRoot's loop records (up to 3664 + 256 bytes into a sector) are not
affected. Nothing else of 0.12 is a ChoralRoot concern.

3d. **The layers' knob row: all layers done 2026-10-07 (FX, PERF, BASS, then KEY, LOOP, METRO).** Every layer hid what
KNOB 1-4 do until a knob turned (a popup). The decided **layer grammar** (`design/choralroot-fm1-fx-screens.png`,
generator `design/make_fx_mockups.py`; the main sheet's state 16): the picker on top and one row of four cells in the
knob colours under it, each a **bespoke glyph that changes with the value**, the cell just turned on a hot block (800
ms, no popup), '–' for a missing parameter. Firmware: `CR_K_KNOBROW` (`cr_screen.h`; `cr_cell_t.pct2`; glyph ids
`CR_G_ROOM MOON ECHOES LFO CLIP SPRING MIX GATE RANGE ARROW SHIFT`, drawn by `cr_draw.c cr_picto`), `cr_ui.c
cu_fx_cells` builds the FX layer (Size room · Damp moon (1 - v) · Type room / spring · Amount mix; Delay echoes (time
index, feedback) · Colour moon; Chorus lfo (rate, depth); Drive clip), the knob handler sets `cu_fx_hot` instead of a
popup; the cache composes only the row's strips on a turn (`perf.sh` (h): UI frame 2875 us avg, was 4124 with the
popups). Fixtures: `tests/gen_cr_screens.py` reads the fx sheet too (states 45-51); `tools/emu/scripts/cr_fx_row.txt`.
**PERF and BASS done** (the fx sheet's states 10, 11, now fixtures 52-53): `cu_perf_cells` (the mode's `CU_PERF_KNOB`
parameters, labels as the popups named them, so ARP reads Division / Direction / Gate / Swing where the mock-up says
Rate / Order / Range / Gate; glyph table in cr_ui.c), `cu_bass_cells` (Behaviour text, Register shift, Sound text, Level
bar), footer "a root: preview · OCT-: back · HOME"; the hot cell is `cu_hot` {layer, knob, until} for all three layers;
tests `cr_perf_row.txt`, `cr_bass_row.txt`. Also: the top line reads "Dub 3.2" while an overdub is merely armed. **KEY,
LOOP, METRO done** (`design/choralroot-fm1-layers-screens.png`, generator `design/make_layers_mockups.py`; the user
chose state 1 for KEY, state 6 for LOOP over 4 / 5 (no ring in the layer), state 7 for METRO; the loop sheet's old state
10 is superseded; 10 is now the count-in): KEY is a knob row whose band is the keyboard (`cr_screen_t.kr_band` 1,
`cr_keyboard` <= 56 px tall under "key"), cells Tonic / Scale / Transpose (shift) / Single (`cu_key_cells`); LOOP the
length picker (stopped) or Overdub / Pause / Undo / Clear (playing) over Sync (range; `CR_CF_DIM` grey while playing) /
Quantize (echoes) / Count-in (gate) / Level (bar) (`cu_loop_cells`), no ring: a playing loop is the corner dial; SAVE
held (loops) a plain picker, no ring; METRO the time signature over Click (bar) and three '–'. No popups in any layer
(OPT + SELECT outside METRO keeps its popup). **The ring** now shows only for the count-in, the undo screen, the capture
states and calibration (since 2026-10-09 not the capture states: 3b). Fixtures 54-57 (the layers sheet's 1 3 6 7); tests
`cr_key_row.txt`, `cr_loop_row.txt`, `cr_metro_row.txt`; `cr_loop_load.txt`'s shot is now `cr_loop_layer_dial` (no ring,
the dial).

3e. **The preset model: done 2026-10-07** (docs/PRESETS.md; mock-ups `design/choralroot-fm1-preset-screens.png`,
generator `design/make_preset_mockups.py`). One editable pool per engine (`cr_bank.c` `pool_count` / `pool_entry` /
`pool_name` / `pool_pos_slot` / `pool_pos_factory` / `pool_pos_new`: 00 INIT, the engine's factory presets each replaced
by the user record bound to it, the added records in slot order); the curated 48-row bank is gone (its trims: `CB_TRIM`
by engine and preset name; PIANO is FM6 F25; FM6 presets 2/5/7/8 renamed FM BELL / FM PAD / FM ORGAN / FM PLUCK on
ChoralRoot). PRESETS / ALGORITHM turn the part's engine's pool (wrapping; ALGORITHM 00 = OFF first), popup "05 / FM PAD
/ FM6 · 05/25" (since 0.16 the count is the presets without INIT, a stripe each: docs/PRESETS.md; it was "05/26") with a
square after an overwritten factory preset (`cr_screen_t.sub_mark`); OPT + PRESETS = the chord part's engine (a
horizontal picker while OPT is held, on release the remembered place per engine, `cs.pool_pos`); the editor's picker
KNOB 1 steps the same pool. SAVE: Overwrite / Save as new picker, then the naming page ("FM6 · 27"); SAVE held 1 s in
the dialog: reset to factory / delete; "no free slot". The binding: `note[15] = 0xA6`, `flags[15]` = factory index + 1
or 0, written by `cb_store` (docs/SOUNDS.md). Settings v6 (`chord_sound` / `bass_sound` = engine << 8 | position,
`pool_pos` per part per engine: the reserve is used up). Also PERF / FX layers: the roots play the chord, SELECT picks;
LOOP Quantize "none" draws no grid. Tests `cr_presets.txt` (wired in test_cr.sh), test_persist's bound FM6 01 after a
relaunch, fixtures 58-62 (the preset sheet's 1 2 3 5 6). **Next**: the Sounds page and the CLI show the binding ("FM6 02
(over FM BELL)" / "FM6 +1"; `install_test.py` / `test_sounds.mjs`), and the main sheet PNG
(`design/choralroot-fm1-screens.png`) re-rendered for states 18 / 23 (their JSON is updated).

3f. **QUAD wired (shown as FM TONE since 3g): done 2026-10-08** (docs/QUAD.md "Status"): engine 15 (`FELUCCA_QUAD`),
shown after FM6; the patch store `quad_store.c` (`OBJ_QUADSTORE` on 0xB2000 / 0xB3000, backup id 22, 2576 bytes); the
Sounds tools' patch kind `quad` (no .syx); the editor's screen plan (`eng_deep_t.screens`: the mock-ups' screens,
docs/EDITOR.md §4 "QUAD"); tests `cr_quad.txt`, `quad_persist_*`, perf (i), regress goldens. The settings record keeps
its eleven engine slots (QUAD's chord place in `rsv_usb`). The BRASS preset's DC (regress health) is fixed by a
per-voice DC blocker after MIX (docs/QUAD.md "DC"); the device build passes (XIP 71.1 %, RAM 81.3 %, POOL 93.8 %) and

presets by ear on the device, the scripts that walk the engine order were shifted by one (OPT + PRESETS +2 to the VA, +4
to the CZ-1; the picker's roots: VA G4, CZ-1 B4).

3g. **FM TONE, ANALOG retired, the engine order: done 2026-10-08** (docs/INTEGRATION.md "0.14: FM TONE, ANALOG
retired"): QUAD shows as FM TONE everywhere the user sees it (code names, files, flags, the patch kind `quad` stay);
ANALOG retired (`FELUCCA_ANALOG` 0: `ENGINES[0]` = `ENG_GONE`; an ANALOG user record plays on the VA with its macros,
`cr_ui.c cu_analog_va`, an added preset of the VA's pool); `ENGINE_ORDER` FM6, FM TONE, VA, CZ-1, PHASE, LOFI, VOICE,
TRIO, WHEEL, PHYS, NOISE (a root each, D4..G5); the bass default VA DEEP SUB; settings v7 (FM TONE's place in ANALOG's
slot 0, `rsv_usb` read once, docs/SETTINGS.md).

3h. **FM TONE aligned with the Digitone manual: done 2026-10-08** (docs/QUAD.md; the review against the manual's
Appendix A): algorithms (the manual's eight routings, its X / Y carriers direct or enveloped), carrier levels, B LEV
law, HARM (−26..+26), Ratio B hands (index B1 × 19 + B2: a detent steps B2, the top of the fraction "B2/B1"; OPT / Shift
steps B1, title hint `fine · B1`), DTUN, FDBK, key scaling (ENV 3: A Key · B1 Key · B2 Key), LFO sync / fade (MULT 1..2k
synced and F1..F2k fixed 120 BPM; FADE bipolar), PHRT (Off All C A+B A+B2), filter chain (the base-width filter before
the multimode; TYPE Off LP12 HP12 LP24); blob v3 (v1, v2 still load; the clients accept 1..3). The UI: `cr_draw.c`
`CR_ALGOS` / `cr_algo_geom` draw the manual's diagrams (dotted direct, solid enveloped carrier lines, the square
feedback loop; all eight rendered and linted by `tests/cr_draw_test.c`), the filter band's OFF / 12 / 24 dB curves
(`wv[7]`), the designer's `ALGOS` and picto the same (FORMAT.md), `design/make_quad_mockups.py` (state 10: ENV 3), the
emulator's `cr_quad.txt` / `cr_quad_ratiob.txt` (B2 moves the timbre, not Y's pitch; B1 the pitch: `wavpitch.py` gained
the spectral centroid). Release INF done the same day: REL 127 = INF on the filter and amp envelopes (`F_TIMEI`,
`engine_t.endless`, panic fades held voices; docs/QUAD.md "Release INF").

## 2026-10-09

3i. **A slot keeps its loop: done 2026-10-09** (the user: "recorded into loop 1, switched to loop 2, switched back: my
pattern is gone"; docs/LOOPER.md "Slots and the record"): leaving a slot saves its changed loop there first (`cr_ui.c
cu_loop_keep` / `cu_loop_flush`): stopped at once ("loop 2 · saved 1"), playing staged in `cu_loop_pend` (3664 B, .pool)
and written at the stop, before another loop-slot write, or at a second switch while playing (a hiccup, traced); a
cleared loop deletes the record; a flash error refuses the switch. `cr_loop_t.loads` lets the UI see a queued switch
cancelled (stop / clear / panic, and now an overdub armed or an undo) and keep the slot number right. Tests:
`cr_loop_test.c s_switch`, `tools/emu/scripts/cr_loop_slots.txt` (test_cr.sh), `loop_persist_check.txt`
(test_persist.sh).

5b. **Boot safety (docs/BOOT-SAFETY.md)**: experiment 1 is answered by the dump (the flash record is not a resume path).
Next, with the Transporter wired and the user's go (each is a deliberate write to a working unit): experiments 2 and 3
(what the SPL does with a bad body sector / with a bad first sector; whether it honours the RAM record after a WDT
reset), then the stub + the loader write order (§5 work items).

3j. **The PLAY menu and the record modes: done 2026-10-09** (docs/LOOPER-MODES.md, approved mock-ups
`design/choralroot-fm1-looper-screens.png`; docs/LOOPER.md "Record modes", "The PLAY menu"). Engine (`cr_loop.[ch]`):
`crl.mode` (`CRL_MODE_*`, posted as `LC_MODE`); Overwrite's clear when the take starts (`crl.ow`, `crl_take_start`);
Replace (`crl.rep`, `rep_n`: the older layers' events reached while a gesture is held are hidden, `CRL_HID` | the hiding
layer in `crl_ev_t.layer`; a sounding one cut by `crl_cut` into a copy in the new layer; `crl.lev0` per layer; undo
restores; `cr_loop_pack` writes live events only); Step (`CRL_CAP_STEP`, `cr_loop_step` / `LP_STEP`: keys up, rest,
back; the steps after the loop's events until `crl_step_commit`); `crl.gen` for the UI's mark cache; `cr_loop_pos`.
Advance's slot jump is the UI's (`cu_rec`: stop, `cu_loop_load(CRL_NEXT_SLOT)`, then REC as Overwrite; from an empty
slot it records there). UI (`cr_ui.c`): `cu_loopmenu` fills `CR_K_LOOPMENU` (the draw agent's `lm_*` fields),
`cu_lm_marks` reads the events under IRQ off only when `crl.gen` or the window changes; PRESETS (`cu_loop_turn` /
`cu_loop_want`, debounced 250 ms while playing), ALGORITHM (`cu_loop_mode_turn`), Keys (`cu_loop_keys`: KNOB 4, GLO tap;
`cu_layer_keys(L_LOOP)` only with Loops), OCT+/- in step entry, the header's "Rec · Overwrite" / "Rep 3.2" / "Rec ·
Step". Snapshot: `lrep lrep_on lstep_g lgen lev0 lstep lstep_end lpos lrep0 lrec_len lstep_len`. Settings v8: `loop_rec`
(the byte `rsv_usb` was): mode bits 0..3, keys bit 4. RAM ~+210 bytes (crl +84, the snapshot +28, the UI's caches ~90),
no POOL. Tests: `cr_loop_test.c` (`s_overwrite`, `s_replace`, `s_step`), `cr_settings_test.c t_loop_rec`,
`tools/emu/scripts/cr_loop_menu.txt` (shots `cr_lm_1..5`) and `cr_loop_modes.txt` (`cr_lm_6 7 9 10 11 12`, the step
loop's WAV), the older loop scripts moved to the menu (SELECT the length, PRESETS the slots, ALGORITHM +2 for the
overdubs, GLO before D#4), test_persist checks the mode and keys. Open: on the device, by ear and hand. Device build
2026-10-09: XIP 73.3 %, RAM 81.8 %, POOL 95.0 % (the slot auto-save's 3664 B pending buffer + the screen's growth); POOL
is the tight resource now.

3k. **The bass part on the second core: built and host-verified; needs the device** (docs/DUALCORE.md). Core 1 started
at power-on (`main.c` before `audio_init`; bring-up `firmware/hal/fm1_cpu1.{h,S}` from fm1-x0x, `.c1_text` in RAMTEXT,
held in `fm1_reboot` / `fm1_enter_uboot` / `fm1_core_reset`); each block core 0 hands it part 1's render (`fx.c
mix_parts_split`, one job, bit for bit one core's samples), parts 0 and 1 get 8 voices each (`voice.c voices_busy_of`);
no answer in 20 ms or a job over 8 ms: one core and the shared budget for the session (`c1 failed` / `c1 gave up` on
GEEK OUT). No setting. The ISR's stage profile (`cr_dbg`: GEEK OUT's fifth line, console `cpu`, perf.sh's `stages`
lines, new scenario (j)). Tests: `tests/cr_cpu1_test.c`, `tools/emu/scripts/cr_dualcore.txt` in test_cr.sh
(`EMU_C1=nostart|hang`, `EMU_STAGES=1`). The hardware checklist: docs/DUALCORE.md, last section.

3l. **Four device findings fixed 2026-10-09 (host-verified; needs the device).** (1) **The loop survives a power
cycle**: `cr_ui.c cu_loop_autosave` writes the changed loop (`crl.dirty`) to its own slot at the stop (LOOP tap, panic,
MIDI stop; a take / overdub committed while playing at that next stop), or after 2 s quiet when it changed while
stopped; never while it plays or a capture is open; "saved to slot N" only when written (docs/LOOPER.md "Slots and the
record"; test_persist `loop_persist_stop` / `_play` / `_back`; `cr_loop_slots.txt` updated: the stopped overdub is
written at its stop). Uses `cu_loop_buf`, no new RAM. (2) **Perform on / off**: PERF tap already toggled `perform_on`
(the LED lit / dim), but in the layer SELECT forces it on and OCT+ only closed it; now `cu_perf_toggle` /
`cu_fx_toggle`: PERF / FX tap with a short message, **OCT+ in the PERF / FX layer toggles**, the layer's label reads
"perform · on / off" ("fx · on / off"), footer "SELECT: mode · OCT+: on/off · OCT-: back". The KNOB 1 cell was kept for
the mode's first parameter (no cell lost); the On/Off is the label. Test `cr_perf_toggle.txt` (the arp's notes out stop
climbing when off). (3) **HOME held 600 ms: the view menu** (`L_VIEW`, `cu_view_menu`, a plain picker: the five views +
"Lock: On/Off", SELECT / white roots, KNOB 1 or the sixth white root the lock, OCT+ ok); HOME tap closes layers / pages
without touching the view and cycles it at home only when unlocked (the default stays unlocked: today's behaviour, the
scripts' HOME taps). Settings v9: the lock in bit 7 of `view` (no reserve was left; `CRS_VIEW` / `CRS_VIEW_LOCK`;
`cr_settings_test t_view_lock`). Test `cr_view_menu.txt`. (4) **The logo**: the idle stripes came back 3 s after the
last chord stopped sounding, and after every PANIC (its cleared chord); now the splash at power-on until the first
input, then the screensaver after 3 min without input (`CR_IDLE_MS` 180000, `cu.last_input`: keys, buttons, knobs, MIDI
channel messages in via `cr_out.c cr_in_msg_n`; a loop playing alone does not count), any input dismisses it
(`cr_panic.txt`, docs/INTEGRATION.md section 5). Open: on the device; the post-PANIC view is black until a chord is
played (it was the stripes); POOL unchanged (no new buffers).

## 2026-10-10

3m. **USB recording fixed: done 2026-10-10** (docs/USB-AUDIO.md "What went wrong and why"). A 14 s recording held 6.5 s
(macOS closed and reopened ChoralRoot In every ~250 ms while sound played). Cause: bit stuffing stretched the 540-byte
packets past the bus time the endpoint reserved (exactly 540) on a USB 2 hub; the fix `wMaxPacketSize` 640
(`UA_EP_MAXP`). Also: the ring held until the host's first read (`ua.cap_hold`), a fixed 44.1-frame packet pattern with
a rare one-frame nudge (Melodee's fill servo removed), the endpoint armed at every service call and from the audio ISR
(`audio.c ua_isr_service`) under `usb_sie_owner`; diagnostics: SysEx 73 DEBUG and `fm1_install.py --debug` (counters,
the last 16 events), `tools/uac_check.py` (ffmpeg + mido). Verified on the device: 12.7 / 13.2 s of 14, 27.9 s of 30,
one open. Tests: `cr_usbaudio_test.c` (render gap, hold, frames byte for byte, the 44-run bound). Later, if hubs still
trouble it: a 4-channel or stereo alternate setting (360 / 180 bytes).

3n. **The screen off when idle: done 2026-10-10 (host-verified; needs the device)** (the user: "leaving the unit on for
a while starts to burn the screen"; docs/INTEGRATION.md section 5 item 7). After 3 min without input the logo (as
before), after 10 min (`cr_ui.c CR_SCREEN_OFF_MS`) the screen off: `lcd.c lcd_power(0)` sends DISPOFF (0x28) and
switches the backlight off (PA2, new `hal/fm1_lcd_hw.h fm1_lcd_backlight`); `cr_ui_draw` builds the model but draws
nothing while off. Any key, button, knob or MIDI channel message in: the whole view redrawn (`cr_draw_invalidate`), then
the backlight and DISPON; the input acts as usual. A loop / the arp / the metronome are not input. `lcd_fill` (main.c's
crash / UBOOT / update screens) lights it again. SysEx **74 SCREEN** 0/1 (`cr_backup.c`, web/EDITOR_PROTOCOL.md,
`fm1_install.py --screen off|on`): off until SysEx on or a local key / button / knob (MIDI in does not wake it).
Emulator: `emu_hal.lcd_dark` (the panel and the shots black), the dump's `lcd:` line (writes, on/off commands). Tests:
`cr_screen_off.txt` (test_cr.sh), `cr_backup_test.c`, `install_test.py`. **On the device 2026-10-10: this made the unit
deaf (it is in the released v0.14 and v0.15): see 3w; the screen off is now a black frame with the backlight on.**

1\. **Done: v0.14 and v0.15 released 2026-10-10** (the user tagged through Claude on request; the rule "never tag
yourself" stands unless the user asks). docs/releases/0.16.md is the next draft (Dual-core hardening, SELECT, the
manager).

3o. **Options > Dual Core On / Off: done 2026-10-10 (host-verified; needs the device)** (docs/DUALCORE.md "Options >
Dual Core"; other FM-1 firmwares' dual-core support reportedly crashes for some people after playing notes). Settings
v11: bit 6 of `view` set = Off (no reserve left; `CRS_DC_OFF` / `CRS_DUALCORE`), older records On. `cr_settings_boot`
sets `cr_cpu1.h cr_c1_off` before `cr_c1_boot`, which then leaves `cr_dbg.c1` at `C1_OFF` (GEEK OUT `c1 off`, `--debug`
`c1_state 0`, the shared budget). Changes take effect at the next power-on (value "On · restart" / "Off · restart",
label "takes effect after a restart"). Tests: `cr_settings_test t_dualcore_v11`, `test_cr.sh` (`cr_dualcore_opt.txt`,
`cr_dualcore_on.txt`, relaunches on one flash file). Open: the device (power cycle with Off: `c1 off`).

3p. **The loop ring's table out of POOL: done 2026-10-10.** `cr_draw.c cr_ring` (6102 B, built at boot by
`cr_ring_build` from `cr_ui.c`) is now a `const` in flash from the generated `firmware/src/cr_ring_table.h`; the builder
lives in `tests/cr_draw_test.c`, which checks the header byte for byte (`CR_RING_GEN=1 sh tests/run_cr_draw.sh` rewrites
it); the boot call is gone. POOL 327012 -> 320908 B of 344064 (95.0 -> 93.3 %), XIP 74.3 -> 75.3 %. The count-in, undo
and calibration screens are pixel-identical (fixtures and emulator shots).

3q. **The web device manager: built 2026-10-10 (host-verified, not run on a device yet)** (docs/DEVICE-MANAGER.md "As
built"): `web/manager.html` + `web/fm1manager.js` (pools per engine in the firmware's order, the ten loop slots with
their header, Place / Keep / Swap / Rename / Delete / Save as a file / Back up / Install a backup / Undo of 20 steps,
every write read back, busy shown as "Stop the loop on the FM-1 first"), `?sim=1` against a simulated FM-1, Web MIDI on
"ChoralRoot FM-1" with hot-plug, the keyboard (ch 1, Bass ch 2). Audition is a program change of the pool position
(ChoralRoot answers no PRESET / UP_LOAD): it plays the row only when the unit is on that engine; an engine select over
MIDI is what is missing. Packs: `tools/make_packs.py` from `sound_templates --packs` (VA, FM6, CZ-1, FM TONE with
patches; the others records). `make_site.py` emits `webapp/installer/manager.html`; links on the landing page and the
installer. Tests: `web/test_manager.mjs` (in `tests/run_tests.sh`). Revised the same day after the first device try
(DEVICE-MANAGER.md "Revision"): Library left / FM-1 right, multi-select, rename on both sides, packs of one's own (Save
selected as a pack, Save all user sounds, file packs as sources), factory rows kept with `factory: true` + position,
drag and drop reorder, the 32-slot capacity shown; sim with 30 sounds. **On the device 2026-10-10: the user tried this
revision with the SELECT firmware (audition, drag reorder, packs, capacity): "quite good now, good for release".** Left:
Melodee's CZ-1 banks as a pack.

3r. **Dual-core reliability, after Melodee PR 21: built 2026-10-10 (host-verified; needs the device)** (docs/DUALCORE.md
"Reliability", docs/releases/0.16.md). With Dual Core On the core rails go up at power-on before core 1 starts
(`hal/fm1_power.h`: SYSVDD 11 -> 14, VDC14 3 -> 4, a step every 100 us, never lower; Off keeps the boot loader's); core
1 runs a request only with the next sequence and a matching check word, never a null fn (`fm1_c1_poll`; misreads counted
in `c1_rejected`); a first misread is counted and core 1 kept, a second or any fault or stall gives core 1 up
(`c1_fallback` 1, GEEK OUT `c1 gave up · Options>Dual Core`); no voltage escalation: the rails are capped at 14 / 4 (the
user's decision after Charles Vestal's review); a fault about core 1 holds it and core 0 resumes (`fm1_vec.S` ->
`fm1_c1_fault_c`; before, a core-1 PC-limit hit reset the chip). `--debug`: `c1_rejected c1_faults c1_fallback rail_*`;
GEEK OUT `· rej n`. Test build `CR_CORE1_TEST` (SysEx 75, `tools/core1_fault_test.py`; the user runs it). Next: the
device checklist in docs/DUALCORE.md. **On the device 2026-10-10 (CR_CORE1_TEST build):** rails read 14/4 (boot 11/3);
`misread` PASS (the second misread gave core 1 up, audio kept running), `null` PASS (refused, core 1 kept); `stall` PASS
and `fault` PASS (core 1 held, audio kept running) in a second run; the script restarts the unit between modes and the
unit re-enumerates slowly after a restart, so run the modes one per invocation or with `--settle 60`. The release build
is back on the unit.

3s. **SysEx 76 SELECT and the reload after a PUT: built 2026-10-10 (host-verified; needs the device)**
(web/EDITOR_PROTOCOL.md): `76 SELECT part engine pos` loads a part's engine and pool position live, as OPT + PRESETS
then PRESETS (rc 1 bad part / engine, 2 bad position, 3 busy: EDIT or SAVE open); `fm1_install.py --select PART ENGINE
POS`. A PUT of a user bank or a patch store reloads a part playing a slot it holds (`crb_reload`): an uploaded DX7 patch
is heard at once. Tests: `tests/cr_backup_test.c`, `tests/install_test.py`, `tools/emu/scripts/cr_select.txt` (the
emulator now hands a completed 7D 46 4C SysEx to `crb_handle`: the `sysex HEX` script command). **On the device
2026-10-10:** `--select` loads chord and bass parts (FM6, FM TONE, VA), a bad position answers rc 2 and a retired engine
rc 1; the manager's audition works on it (the user).

3t. **POOL trim: done 2026-10-10 (on the device).** `engines.c eng_state[ENG_STATE_NPART]`: the engines' state for the
chord and bass parts only (2 with `FELUCCA_SEQ` 0); the accessors return 0 for a part without state and FM6 / WHEEL /
CZ-1 / PHYS return early (part 3's idle FM6 block code no longer runs). POOL 320908 -> 295132 B (93.3 -> 85.8 %), 35
WAVs bit-identical. Candidates left (the agent's survey): the store mirrors (`czu fm6u fm6_bank va_store quad_store
cz_bank_cache`, ~21 KB: a design change, read from flash), `cv_px` 124 -> 120 rows (1920 B), `cz_patch[NTRK]` (288 B);
RAM (86.1 % by the size line): `trk[2..3]`, `fm6_patch[NTRK]`, `fm6_eff[NTRK]` for parts that never sound.

3u. **Dual Core Off by default: done 2026-10-10 (on the device)** (the user: enough crash reports on other firmwares'
dual core; docs/DUALCORE.md, docs/SETTINGS.md v12, docs/releases/0.16.md). Settings v12: bit 6 of `view` set = **On**
(`CRS_DC_ON`); every record older than v12 loads Off whatever its bit said (also a restored 0.14 / 0.15 backup);
`cr_cpu1.h cr_c1_off = 1` until a record says On. `test_cr.sh` and `perf.sh` turn it on through Options for the
dual-core checks; perf.sh prints both (single core worst half 43 % of the budget, scenario (g)). On the unit after the
flash: `c1_state 0`, audio clean (`uac_check` 12.74 s of 14, no gaps). Also: `tests/mod_test.c` compiles again (it had
stopped `tests/run_tests.sh` before the regress goldens, target budget and web tests: all pass now, 197 goldens
unchanged); `target_budget.txt`'s `fm1_timer5_irq` follows the USB Record fix (9814504).

3v. **The boot stub: done 2026-10-10, stub v3 on the device, the rescue key and a real cable pull confirmed by hand**
(docs/BOOT-SAFETY.md "Experiment 2, answered" and "As built"). Experiment 2 on the unit: a package with stale app.bin /
app_area_head CRCs boots warm and cold, so the SPL verifies nothing over the body. Built: `firmware/stub/` (app.bin's
first 0xEE0 bytes: watchdog, PA2 low, a 48 ms key window judged by its tail, the commit marker at 0x91FE0 with a CRC-32
of the body, then the app at XIP 0x02001000, or the staged loader by the RAM record + core reset, or UBOOT on a second
rescue attempt / no loader; three short flashes = loader, one long = UBOOT; PA2 high again before a hand-over), the
loader's order (marker sector erased first, written last; sector 0x4000 only when it differs; `fm1pkg_make.py` keeps
sector 0x4000 byte-identical across builds by compensation bytes; `firmware/stub/stub.lock` pins the stub's hash and
`--release` stops on a mismatch), the app keeps its staged loader (`ota.c ota_staged` / `ota_boot_cleanup`),
`tests/cr_stub_test.c`, `tests/stub_pkg_test.py`, `tests/ldr_test.c` (a cut after every erase and page: 0 unprotected
with the same stub, 16 while the stub's own sector is written), SysEx 77 STUB and `fm1_install.py --stub test|rescue`,
DEBUG's `stub_*` values (the key samples too), `tools/make_stub_test_pkg.py bad-marker`. **On the unit:** v1 booted in
17.2 ms and did not see OCT- at power-on; v2 (the 48 ms window, the samples recorded): 60.8 ms, `--stub test` -> "staged
loader ok", `--stub rescue` -> update mode (ota-FM-1_900), left by installing, then "rescue attempts before this boot
1"; the bad-marker package -> update mode after the restart, the good build installed from there (the refusal of a body
without a commit, from a warm restart). **The cause of the missed key is PA2 (3w)**: the stub runs before the app pulls
PA2 low, and with PA2 high the key matrix / 595 chain is dead. v3 drives PA2 low first: flashed, boots in 60.8 ms, `PA
in 0x1E1` (PA2 low). **Confirmed by hand on v3 the same day (BOOT-SAFETY.md "On the unit, 2026-10-10"):** OCT- alone at
power-on -> three flashes, a black screen, update mode (the key, the flashes, the cold hand-over); OCT- + OCT+ ->
calibration; **the cable pull** (an install cut at 5 %, the unit switched off and on: three flashes, update mode, the
same install finished it). Left: the second attempt -> one long flash and UBOOT (needs a power cycle to leave). The
release workflow's native build must give the same stub bytes as the Docker build (`gh` is not installed on this Mac:
the user runs release.yml by workflow_dispatch, build only, version 0.16-dev, or it shows at the tag). Note: the
session's permission classifier lets Claude flash normal builds after the user's say-so but refused the deliberately
stale-CRC package; the user ran that one from the app's Run button (the bad-marker package went through).

3w. **PA2 is the backlight AND the key matrix's enable; the idle screen-off of 0.14 / 0.15 made the unit deaf: fixed
2026-10-10 (on the unit: with the screen black a key sounds and wakes it; the user: "return looks fine").** Seen on the
unit: after 10 idle minutes the screen went dark and no key sounded or woke it; `--screen on` over MIDI brought screen
and keys back. `lcd_power(0)` drove PA2 high (`fm1_lcd_backlight(0)`); with PA2 high nothing on the 595 chain works
(keys, buttons, knobs, LEDs). Fix: `fm1_lcd_backlight` and `lcd_power` are gone, PA2 is driven low once in
`fm1_lcd_hw_init` and never raised (`hal/fm1_lcd_hw.h` states the fact), the screen off is `cr_draw.c cr_draw_black()`
(one black frame, the backlight on, no DISPOFF), the wake a full redraw; the emulator models PA2 (`emu_hal.pa2`: high =
no keys, LEDs or picture; script command `pa2 high|low`; `cr_pa2.txt`, `cr_screen_off.txt` and test_cr.sh fail on the
old code). **v0.14 and v0.15 are public with the bug; v0.16 fixes it.** To look at on the unit: the black frame's glow,
the 80 ms paint on going off and on waking, keys / knobs / buttons with `--screen off`, the real 10-minute idle-off.
Idea if the glow bothers: none without hardware unknowns; PWM on PA2 would switch the matrix too.

3x. **The player's manual, the landing page, the host suite, small fixes: done 2026-10-10.** `docs/MANUAL.md` (every
gesture checked against cr_ui.c; README refreshed; the rescue key and the interrupted update are confirmed on the unit,
the caveats removed); `web/site/index.html` at 0.16 (not published; the two editor panel pictures `panel-editor-osc.png`
/ `panel-editor-filter.png` need the designer); `tests/run_tests.sh` ends `ALL HOST TESTS PASSED` (text spacing was the
library path lost under /bin/sh; the FM6 bank / editor checks rewritten to what the code does; a verdict line always);
Options > Hold Time alone is now saved (`cr_settings.c crs_hold_seen`; test_persist); documents brought in line with the
code (PLAN.md's facts, SETTINGS, EDITOR, LOOPER-MODES, the emulator's README, INSTALL-COMPAT: the stub is from 0.16 on);
**the sound read-out counts the presets without INIT** (the user's request: `PHASE · 06/06` lights six of six stripes,
INIT none; `cr_ui.c cu_pool_popup` / `cu_pool_lit`, a long meter fills in the time of 14 stripes, `cr_draw.c
CR_FILL_MAX`; the designer's PNG sheets for those states are stale).

3y. **`tools/fm1_install.py` rewritten onto python-rtmidi workers (no mido): done 2026-10-10, soaked on the unit, on
main.** The old tool could hang forever closing a port (python-rtmidi's callback waits for the GIL inside CoreMIDI's
delivery while the main thread waits in MIDIPortDispose: any message arriving during a close; MIDI Clock Out is on by
default). The rewrite polls in worker processes (one per link, started by posix_spawn; ports always closed by their
worker; a 60 s no-step watchdog; `leave()` ends within 5 s). Its first version (the one in the tag v0.16) killed workers
with ports open as the unit restarted and twice left the FM-1's MIDI port offline in CoreMIDI until `MIDIRestart()`; the
second version closes first, names an offline port in its error and has `--rescan-midi`. **Soak on the unit:** twelve
`--stub test` (every one exit 0, the port back each time), `--stub rescue`, two real installs (one out of update mode,
one app -> loader -> app), `--backup` and `--restore` of the same file with its restart, a 60 s `uac_check`, repeated
`--debug` / `--info`, no process left behind. **Still true:** in 3 of 13 restart requests the reply to 77 STUB did not
reach the Mac although the unit restarted (the tool now says "no answer was seen" and judges by the restart: exit 0).
Likely the firmware: `cr_backup.c ed_service` restarts 150 ms after the request once `so_r == so_w`, i.e. once the reply
is in the endpoint FIFO, not once the host has taken it (the same for RESTART after a restore): make the restart wait
for the IN packet.

3z. **USB Record over an hour: measured 2026-10-10** (`uac_check.py OUT.wav 3600 --nonotes`, the Mac busy with builds).
The unit: open the whole hour apart from one restart of the stream by macOS, 3 597 711 packets for 3 597 791 SOFs, 0
underruns, 12 overruns (189 frames = 4 ms lost; nine of them in two minutes of heavy host load, three at stream closes),
`ua_nudges_up` 69 036: the FM-1's audio clock is about +435 ppm against the Mac's USB clock (44 119 Hz), absorbed by the
nudges. The WAV was 12 % short, but ffmpeg's avfoundation capture is short by the same amount on the Onyx interface
(56.70 s of 60 on both), so `uac_check`'s drain ratio measures ffmpeg; judge the unit by `ua_frames_last_open` /
`ua_overruns` (uac_check's text and USB-AUDIO.md "Measuring" should say so: not yet changed). Worth repeating once on an
idle Mac, and by ear in a DAW.

3y2. **0.17 dev, 2026-10-10 (on the unit):** the missed 77 / RESTART replies were the firmware: `crb.reboot = fm1_ms |
1u` is one ms ahead when `fm1_ms` is even, so `fm1_ms - crb.reboot > 150u` passed at once in one request of four and the
unit detached before the host's IN token. Now `cr_backup.c crb_leave`: the reply's last packet taken by the host (`usb.c
so_gone`, TxPktRdy), 5 ms grace, a floor of 150 ms and a bound of 500 ms from the request (`tests/cr_restart_test.c`;
docs/releases/0.17.md). On the unit: 26 `--stub test`, every reply seen (0.16: 3 missed of 13). **Still open: twice in
those 26 back-to-back restarts the FM-1's MIDI port stayed offline in CoreMIDI afterwards** (the tool names it;
`--rescan-midi` brings it back), so killing workers was not the (only) cause; it never happened with the old tool, which
kept its port open across the restart, nor in today's installs. `tools/uac_check.py` now judges by the unit's counters
(USB-AUDIO.md "Measuring"). The landing page has a full-width orange "Device manager" button in "Get it" and the
installer page a button-like link (not published: the pages workflow by hand, version 0.16). The unit runs the 0.17 dev
build.

3y3. **The dancing arp is back on the Chord view: 0.17 dev, 2026-10-10 (flashed; the user to look).** The user's request
after a review video of an older firmware (the arp screen `CR_K_ARP` was dropped by b25168b, i.e. from 0.14 on).
`cr_ui.c cu_arp_on`: the Chord view only; Perform on in a clocked mode (Arpeggiate, Arp 2 Octaves, Pattern; not Strum,
Slop, Harp); while the chord's voice is stepping (`cr_snap.perf_step` from `cr_engine.c cr_perform_stepping`: also with
LATCH, and with a loop driving it); Options > Motion not Off; a 150 ms linger after the voice ends so re-fingering does
not flash the big chord (`CU_ARP_LINGER_MS`: 0 for strict). A hop redraws 3 of 6 strips (3.6 ms). Also fixed: PANIC
cleared the LATCH LED but not the engine's latch (`CRE_STICKY 0`). Tests `cr_arp_view.txt`, test_cr.sh, cr_engine_test,
cr_draw_test.

3y4. **Layers, the KEY layer, the PERF layer's arp band, the LOCK layer: 0.17 dev, 2026-10-10 (flashed; the user to
try).** The user's requests. (1) The arp screen no longer depends on Options > Motion (his unit had Motion Off = 2: that
hid it; `tools/emu/seed_settings.py` seeds an emulator flash from a unit's record, `cr_arp_unit.txt`). (2) OCT+ closes a
layer that has nothing to confirm (PERF, FX, PLAY menu; it was the on/off toggle in PERF / FX), and a layer's own button
held again closes it; taps keep the on/off; footers name the printed buttons. (3) KEY layer: MAJ / MIN pick the scale
with LOCK on too without touching the latch (`cu.kpick`), MAJ / MIN glow half, the selected one full (Stock LEDs: only
the selected lit); a root alone keeps the scale. (4) KEY layer keys pick / play on an OPT tap (OPT never opens Options
there), settings v13 `loop_rec` bit 5. (5) PERF layer: the top half is the arp while notes are stepped; the picker for 2
s on entry and after each SELECT detent. (6) LOCK layer (B3 held; B3 now toggles on release): KNOB 1 `Type key` Clears /
Keeps, KNOB 2 `Ext keys` Toggle / Pushed (a push = keys down until all up; the set {K} pushed again as K alone clears;
otherwise the pushed keys replace the set), `loop_rec` bits 6 / 7 (that byte is full; an older firmware reading a v13
record resets it). Stale designer sheets: fx-screens, layers-screens; no mock-up of the LOCK layer or the arp band.

## 2026-10-11

3y5. **LOCK's top row, three LED levels with Key Mode's scale lights, KEY layer page 2 (Key plays, Keyboard), Single
Full / Split Oct / Split Bass: 0.17 dev, 2026-10-11, host-verified, NOT flashed (the user is trying another firmware and
said not to flash; the build is saved, flash when he says the unit is back).** (A) With LOCK, the one latched type
pushed again alone goes off (single notes); the latched extensions stay. (B) `hal/fm1_input.h fm1_led_dim2` /
`FM1_LED_DIM2_NS` 9600 (the tonic) beside `FM1_LED_DIM_NS` 3200 (scale notes, every idle glow); with Key Mode on the
in-scale roots glow low, the tonic mid, out-of-scale dark, sounding full; Stock LEDs: scale keys lit. A mid pulse longer
than the 595 shift is busy-waited in the TIMER5 ISR on the columns with a tonic LED: measure on the unit (console `inp`:
`dim2_bits`, `tick_cost_max_us`). (C) In the KEY layer SELECT turns the page (no tempo there); page 2: KNOB 1 Key plays
Chords / Notes (Notes: as Key off, out-of-scale roots do nothing), KNOB 2 Keyboard Chords / Full (27 keys chromatic, no

(today's Split) / Split Bass (keys below the split play the bass part by hand; Chords layout: the pitch-class split

on one comes up on its default. **It freed 12.8 KB of flash and NO RAM or POOL** (the five had no state): the memory for

(keep MODAL / MEMB, a Karplus-Strong string of ~1 KB a voice, 6 voices): the user decides. POOL free today: 48 864 B;
the delay line is 131 072 B (65 536 mono samples), the reverb ~11.9 KB. `tests/target_budget.txt`'s `fm1_alnk0_irq`

3y11. **Switch off, switch on: where you left off; the update gesture: 0.18 dev, 2026-10-11 (flashed; the user to

Bass on (the old "off at every power-on" rule is superseded), part levels and pans, the FX state by tag

OCT+ + HOME held 5 s** = update mode through the stub (`cr_backup.c cr_update_poll`; without a stub: USB boot); OCT- + OCT+
alone is PANIC and nothing more. Flash map: 0xB4000..0xB5FFF are no longer free.

3y12. **The effects rework: 0.18 dev, 2026-10-11 (flashed overnight; the user to listen).** `firmware/src/cr_fx.c`
(`CR_FX 1`; Felucca's effects stay behind 0, its goldens unchanged), docs/FX.md. Both new effects run at 22.05 kHz
through a polyphase half-band pair. **Delay**: two int16 lines of 1.000 s, a gliding cubic read (tape-style bend, no
click), tone + low cut + tanh in the loop, feedback to 0.97, Stereo / Ping-pong, Spread (R a part of L: synced 1:1 3:4
2:3 1:2, free x1.00..x0.50), sync 1/32..1/2 with dotted and triplets or 21..1000 ms, a wow. **Reverb**: an 8-line FDN
with a Hadamard mix, lines 57..125 ms scaled by Size, modulated, damped, RT60 0.3..72 s and "Inf", pre-delay, low cut,
four input all-passes, width. FX memory 130 156 B (was 142 940): POOL 82.4 %, 60.6 KB free. **Global effects**: a sound
carries only its sends; the parameters live in the session by tag. The FX layer's SELECT steps Reverb 1, Reverb 2,
Chorus, Delay 1, Delay 2, Drive; KNOB 4 on page 1 is the chord part's Send; seven new glyphs. FX off now closes the bass
part's sends too. CPU by the emulator: both on 233 us of 2902 (8 %), worst perf block 48..51 %; on the unit at idle
`--debug` reads `st_fx_us 85`: measure with sends up. A save waits for the tail to fall under -60 dBFS, at most 30 s
(`CRS_TAIL_MS`), then cuts a 46 ms hole: the user's call. Review WAVs and plots: ../fx-review/. The web emulator is not
rebuilt; the designer does not know the new glyphs. `tests/target_budget.txt` was re-baselined.

3y13. **0.18 dev, 2026-10-11 (flashed; the user to try): saving is manual; the PLAY menu's pages and Rate; the loop
editor; effects round 2.** (1) **SAVE held = the global save** (settings block's manual fields, the session, the loop in
RAM; "saved"; each object written is a 45 ms gap if sound rings; deferred to the stop while a loop plays); **GLO + SAVE
held 1 s = revert** to the last save ("reverted"); a tap is the preset dialog; the SAVE-held loop map is gone. Automatic
still: the Options entries and the calibration (docs/SETTINGS.md "Manual and automatic" has every field's side). Loops
are written only by the global save or on leaving their slot with unsaved changes (`cu_loop_autosave` removed); an
unsaved slot shows a small square. (2) PLAY menu: SELECT turns the page (1: Length, Rate, Quantize, Count-in; 2: Level);
Rate x1/4 x1/2 x3/4 x1 x1.5 x2 x4 (the loop's own clock in quarter-ticks, a change taken at the loop's start, stored in
the session); Keys only on GLO; Step with Length Free is exactly its steps, a step = the Quantize value (a quarter when
none). (3) **Loop editor** (EDIT in the PLAY menu): the grid is Quantize; SELECT / KNOB 1 the step, keys replace it (the
cursor stays), REC clears, KNOB 2 step length in cells, KNOB 3 loop length in steps, KNOB 4 velocity; the first edit
folds the layers (no undo after: GLO + SAVE is the way back). (4) Effects round 2 (the user: "a lot cleaner", still
pops): the delay's drop-out was rounding in the loop (fixed), delay at 44.1 kHz with 0.743 s a side, reverb at 22.05
kHz, sends are the parts' (global), the picker's roots always play, SysEx 78 PEAK (per-stage worst cases, cleared on
read; `--debug` prints them). **On the unit:** reverb 331..345 us a half (3x the emulator; `CR_FX_RAM=1` made no
difference), delay 74..168, CZ-1's part peaks 1700..1985 of 2902, and halves are counted late although they finish
inside the budget (`pk_late_us 896`): the ISR starts late; round 3 is with the effects agent (latency instrumentation, a
cheaper reverb, the shed). POOL is 95.0 % (17.2 KB free).

## The hand-over's summary at the split (2026-10-11)

The standing paragraphs of the old `docs/HANDOFF.md` as they were when the file was split, for the record: parts of them
were already stale then (the first says both that the unit runs v0.17's firmware and v0.16's; the verification counts
are of 2026-10-10). Its table of repositories, its working rules and its "facts that are easy to get wrong" went into
the new `docs/HANDOFF.md`, corrected.

**Where things stand.** v0.14, v0.15, v0.16 (2026-10-10) and v0.17 (2026-10-11) are released. The unit runs v0.17's
firmware (the local dev build of the same sources). The user's FM-1 is healthy, plugged into the Mac by USB, and runs
the firmware of v0.16 (the local build of 9c7445c, byte-identical to the release package). Everything since v0.15 is on
main, host-verified and flashed: items 3p..3z below. The user's Fable session ran out of weekly usage; this file is the
hand-over to an Opus 5.5 session.

**Rules learned this week, on top of the working rules below:** (1) **only Claude flashes the FM-1**; subagents never
run `tools/fm1_install.py` or the Transporter tools: they build and say "build ready", Claude flashes and checks
`--info`, one successful `--info` between flashes, never while another session (e.g. the PurpleMonkey one) may write the
unit; (2) a full battery / USB power and an awake computer before every install; an interrupted install bricks the unit
(recoverable only with the Transporter: MvaveFM1Unbricker `TRANSPORTER-GUIDE.md`); (3) the device can be measured from
the Mac without the user: `python3 tools/fm1_install.py --debug` (SysEx 73: 80+ counters and the last 16 USB events),
`--screen off|on` (74), `--select PART ENGINE POS` (76), `tools/uac_check.py` (records ChoralRoot In with ffmpeg while
playing chords over MIDI), `tools/core1_fault_test.py` (needs a CR_CORE1_TEST build); (4) never raise the core rails
above SYSVDD 14 (1.35 V) / VDC14 4 (1.45 V) (the user's decision after Charles Vestal's review of Melodee PR 21).

**Next up (the user's order):** (a) done: the device manager passed on the unit (the live site waits for the user's
"publish"; the landing page is at 0.16 on main); (b) done: the boot stub v3, confirmed by hand with a real cable pull
(3v); the screen-off fix confirmed (3w): released as v0.16; (c) FM TONE presets by ear with the user; (d) the POOL: 85.8
% after 3t, the store mirrors next if more is needed; (e) the open items 4..6 below; the owner's review list below. The
unit runs v0.16's firmware.

**Owner's review list (behaviour found while writing the manual; nothing changed, the user decides):** OCT- + OCT+ held
5 s shows "update mode" but enters the ROM's UBOOT (the WL82 disk, useless to the installer): with the stub it could ask
for the rescue instead (`fm1_stub_request(STUB_REQ_RESCUE)`: the path proven on the unit); the BASS layer's footer says
"a root: preview" but the white roots pick the Behaviour silently; BASS + EDIT works only with BASS held, not with its
layer locked open; Level and Pan belong to the part and are saved nowhere (bass level 102 came back as 92); the FX
layer's KNOB 1-3 values and picked effect, the voicing and the octave are not remembered; every sound change replaces
the FX amounts with the preset's; Raw Chord Sound On with no performance mode doubles every note; MIDI Raw Chord steps
from Off to ch 1 (the default 3 is never offered); step entry with LOCK latched does not advance (OCT+ after every
chord); ALGORITHM one click from off gives 00 INIT while a BASS tap gives DEEP SUB; SAVE held > Delete on the current
slot says "deleted" but the loop stays loaded; SELECT in the KEY layer is the tempo (every other layer: the picker);
"Key: C" is hidden while a loop plays; Advance outside the PLAY menu: "next: slot N" is replaced at once by "rec: play
to start"; calibration calls SEL "SCL"; the view is black after PANIC until a chord; in the editor ENV held + EDIT jumps
to the bass sound's editor; the modulation mark is also blue for velocity / key / random / mod wheel (the manual names
three colours).

### What is done (all on the device, commit 4ec3f42)

Milestones M0–M6 complete; M7 partly. The instrument: chord block on the keybed with B3 = LOCK, roots D4–G5, layers that
lock open on hold (OCT−/HOME close), Key Mode, play styles, performance modes, bass, FX, the looper, Options one setting
per screen, views incl. SCOPE and GEEK OUT, calibration, splash, MIDI in/out with clock both ways, backup/restore over
SysEx (installer page + `fm1_install.py --backup/--restore`), the sound editor (groups → screens → lanes, SELECT wraps,
SHIFT tap/hold, picker preview with OCT− cancel, quick mod mapping by holding ENV/LFO, no slide animations), engines:
ANALOG PHASE LOFI VOICE TRIO WHEEL PHYS NOISE, ChoralRoot's **VA** (4 osc with Basic/Morph/Noise modes, morphing filter
with stereo spread, 4 env, 4 LFO, 8-slot matrix, patch per user slot), Melodee's **FM6** (Dexed-exact, 24 factory, DX7
SysEx, patch per slot, capped at 8 voices) and **CZ-1** (Casio's 64 tones, 8 banks, Casio SysEx, patch per slot), the
all-synth cut (SAMPLE/GRAIN/DRUM/sequencer removed), the performance pass (deferred saves, smoothed limiter, faded
steals, cheap ring and rasterisers), USB audio **capture only** ("ChoralRoot In": master + CHORD + BASS, 6 ch; Options
USB Record / USB Level; the serial console only when USB Record is Off or in SAFE MODE), the boot guard rework (power-on
never counts; two crash boots → SAFE MODE; ROM boot after four; `boot` console command; GEEK line), Felucca 1.0.3.1's
divide-by-zero brick fix (fm4_convert.c + div0 trap off) and 1.0.5.1's sub-bass fix, the installer guard (refuses Sloop
/ Felucca betas / unknown firmwares with an override; see INSTALL-COMPAT.md: the loader was never the cause), the
website + installer on Pages, releases via Actions.

Released: **v0.17 (2026-10-11, tagged 3bac899 by Claude at the user's word)**:
https://github.com/Quixotic7/ChoralRootFM1/releases/tag/v0.17 (docs/releases/0.17.md: the arp screen back, note-name and
scale lights, KEY page 2 with Key plays / the Full keyboard / Split Bass, the LOCK layer, one rule for OCT- / OCT+,
Reset Settings, fixes) after **v0.16 (2026-10-10, the user tagged 9c7445c)**:
https://github.com/Quixotic7/ChoralRootFM1/releases/tag/v0.16 (the deaf-after-idle fix, the boot stub, Dual Core Off by
default, the manual; docs/releases/0.16.md; the release build's package is byte-identical to the local build that is on
the unit, sha256 3aac9ffe..., so GitHub's native build made the same stub; Pages refreshed with the 0.16 landing page;
the published text still begins "ChoralRoot 0.16 (in progress)": edit that line on the release page) after **v0.15
(2026-10-10)**: https://github.com/Quixotic7/ChoralRootFM1/releases/tag/v0.15 (Options > Dual Core) after **v0.14
(2026-10-10)**: https://github.com/Quixotic7/ChoralRootFM1/releases/tag/v0.14 (docs/releases/0.14.md, 0.15.md). The dev
version string is now "ChoralRoot 0.18" (choralroot.c); docs/releases/0.18.md is the next draft (not yet created).

## 2026-10-12

3y14. **0.18 dev (flashed; the user to try).** The effects, round 3 (the user: "the reverb and delay are very good"): the
audio ISR clears the half's pending bit before the render (a late half used to swallow the next boundary's interrupt and
the DAC replayed an old half: the pops; `CR_ACK_LATE=1` builds the old order), both effects block-wise (counted ~290 us a
half for both; not yet read on the unit with sound), SysEx 78 PEAK grown (`stale_halves`, `pk_lag_us`, `irq_gap_*`,
`key_eng_us`...). The Size glyph's room opens up with Size; the delay's Time is faster clockwise. Keys that play are read
from inside a redraw (`cr_ui_pump` through `cr_draw_idle`). CZ-1's definitions cached per patch and its lines rendered by
specialised loops (counted 1980 -> 630 us for eight voices, bit-exact), parts 2 and 3 not mixed, Felucca's FX-hold layer
out (`FELUCCA_PERFORM 0`), the master and mix loops, `--gc-sections`; RAM 81.6 %. **Step mode and the loop editor are a
step sequencer** (the user: "more like an Elektron"): a step is a sixteenth of the loop's time, 1..64 steps, Free = the
steps entered, a new root is a new step also with LOCK latched, the editor opens on any loop, KNOB 2 is the note's length
and moves nothing, four rows of 16 on the screen. HANDOFF.md was split from this file; 19 merged worktrees were removed
(their branches remain: the hook blocks branch deletion).

## 2026-10-13

3y15. **0.18 dev (built, not flashed).** The loop editor grew a second page and the steps their own settings (the user:
"The loop editor note length is minimum of 1 step, it would be good if we could go lower like 0.75, 0.5, 0.25 steps. And
also maybe do a ratchet on a step we can enable. Conditional steps would also be good ... let the preset knob change
selected step, select change pages. Algo knob to change the voicing per step. Also while in this menu, let the octave
keys work ... hold GLO and turn steps to snap to 8, 16, 32, 48, and 64 steps. And option + turn select step knob to jump
+- 8 steps"; then, the same day: Chance on page 1 in place of the 25 / 50 / 75 % conditions, KNOB 1 no longer a cursor).
PRESETS is the cursor (GLO held: 8 steps), SELECT the page, ALGORITHM the step's voicing, OCT- / OCT+ the octave; HOME
and EDIT leave. Step: Note len (1/4 1/2 3/4, 1 .. 64) / Steps (GLO held: 8 16 32 48 64) / Chance / Velocity. Trig:
Ratchet / Condition / Voicing. Storage: `crl_data_t.st`, two bytes a step for the first 64 steps (RAM +352 B, POOL +8 B,
flash +3.5 KiB); the record gets flag bit 1 and the entries after the events inside the old `CRL_REC_MAX`, so a loop
without them packs byte for byte as before and 0.14 .. 0.17 load one with them (and play it plain). A ratchet stays on
its one loop voice; conditions are a table on the pass mod 12; a chance is one seedable roll per step per pass
(docs/LOOPER.md "Ratchet, condition, chance, voicing"). **The preset knob in the PLAY menu** ("sometimes in the play menu
turning the preset knob ends up changing the preset, which is a bug, but not sure how to repro"): the menu's own button
held again past the hold time closes the menu, so LOOP held + PRESETS with the menu already open chose a slot when
turned within 300 ms and reached the chord sound's pool when turned later; a knob turned while that button is still down
now opens the layer again and acts in it (`cu.hclosed`; `tools/emu/scripts/cr_loop_presetknob.txt` fails on 722fe71).
Not done: the ISR cost is estimated from the source, not counted in the disassembly; the snap in 3/4 and 6/8 has no
script; `web/fm1backup.js`'s comment on byte 7 does not name bit 1 (the manager reads neither).


**2026-10-12, the sends: one value, and the FX layer's Sends page** (0.18 dev; built, not flashed; number it at the
merge). The user: "if I am in the FX menu from main and change the FX sends, then I enter the sound editor and go to
the FX send page, the send values shown [do not] update to what they actually are until I turn them", and: "when you
enter the FX menu from main the first page should also show the 4 FX send levels for convenience". The cause: two
stores for the chord part's sends. `cs.fx_amt[]` holds the amounts; the track's `P_DIST .. P_REV` are what the audio
reads, written by `cu_fx_apply` as the amounts or as 0 with FX off. The FX layer read the first, the editor the
second: with FX off (a FX tap, in its layer too) the editor's FX group read 0 % four times and its knob stepped from
0, switched FX on and the other three jumped back. With FX on the two agreed (no case found in the emulator where
they did not, on any of the six engines). FM TONE's AMP Drive wrote the track alone, so the next `cu_fx_apply` took
it back. The fix: `cu_send` / `cu_send_set` (`cr_ui.c`) are the only way the UI reads or writes a send (the FX
layer, KNOB 4 of the main view, CC 91 / 93 / 94, the editor through `ce_param` / `ce_knob`, `ce_send_ref` naming the
cells that are sends); the chord track's four fields are `cu_fx_apply`'s output and nothing reads them back. No
audio change (`cu_fx_apply` writes what it wrote; fx.c untouched). The Sends page: `CU_FXP[0]`, Drive / Chorus /
Delay / Reverb on KNOB 1..4 with the editor's names, order, step and per-cent read-out, the chord part's as every
Send cell; `cs.fx_page == CU_FXPG_SENDS`, `cs.fx_sel` stays (KNOB 4 of the main view is still the last effect's
send); the session's `fx.sel` gets the tag `CSS_FXE_SENDS` (6), older records open on the effect's page they name.
The Send cells of Reverb 1, Chorus, Delay 1 and Drive are now redundant and were left in place. Left for the user's
decision: a Send cell reads 0..99 and steps 4, the Sends page and the editor read per cent of 127 and step 6 (57 is
"44" on Reverb 1 and "45%" on Sends); with FX off the bass part's drive still sounds while the chord part's does
not (fx.c gates the three sends, not the drive). `tools/emu/scripts/cr_fx_sends.txt`, the dump's `editor fx` lines,
`design/make_fx_mockups.py` state 15, the manual's `layer-fx-sends.png`.

**0.18 dev, 2026-10-11 (not flashed): the Size glyph's inner box; the slow loop Rates.** Two notes from the owner.
(1) "Reverb size rendering is wrong. Revert that glyph. Currently it's now scaling the whole icon. I don't want that.
The glyph renders an inner box and outer box like a room, I only want the inner box to change size. The inner box
should be small when the room size is small and large when the room size is large." `CR_G_ROOM` (`cr_draw.c
cr_picto`; Size on Reverb 1 is its only user) is the drawing of before `1121f93` again, the box fixed at the glyph
box, with the far wall's direction turned round: 22 % of the box at Size 0, 70 % at 127 (it was 70 -> 22 % at first,
then the whole icon 55 -> 100 %). The delay's Time is untouched. `tests/cr_draw_test.c` "room" checks that the box
does not move, that the far wall grows and that 3 px or more of background stay between them; the manual's
`layer-fx.png` is new. (2) "Loop Rate is good, but it would be good to go slower, 1/8, 1/12, 1/16 as it can be nice to
make it so each step is a whole bar for long loops." The Rate has eleven values: x1/16 x1/12 x1/8 x1/6 x1/4 x1/2 x3/4
x1 x1.5 x2 x4 (x1/6 is ours: the value between x1/4 and x1/8). The loop's clock advances in 48ths of a tick a tempo
tick (`CRL_RATE_Q48`; quarters before), the engine's index is in the knob's order (x1 = 7), and the session's
`loop_rate` is steps from x1 as it was, now -7 .. 3: no stored value moved, and a 0.18 dev build of before reads the
four new ones as x1. Nothing else in the looper changed, because every time of a loop's content is in the loop's own
ticks (docs/LOOPER.md "The slow rates": what was checked, and the one cost, a hand-played take's resolution of 83 ms
at x1/16 and 120 BPM). Tests: `cr_loop_test.c` `s_rate` over 48 bars for all eleven and the new `s_slow`,
`cr_settings_test.c`, `tools/emu/scripts/cr_loop_rate.txt`. Seen on the way: `docs/manual/shots.txt` no longer gives
the committed `loop-editor.png` / `loop-editor-trig.png` byte for byte (the cursor's blink phase at the shot: main's
pictures show it off, a run today shows it on); they were left as they are.

3y16. **PHYS retired; the preset slots looked at, not changed: 0.18 dev, 2026-10-13 (built, not flashed).** The user:
"PHYS isn't the greatest engine and has built in chord modes which conflict with the purpose of ChoralRoot so I think
we should get rid of it, much of these sounds can be done with FM too. On the fence about removing wheel, keep it for
now." and "Cut Phys engine." Done as 3y10's five: `FELUCCA_PHYS` 0 in ChoralRoot's units (Felucca's suite keeps it,
the three files stay in the tree), engine 9 is `ENG_GONE`, shown: FM6, FM TONE, VA, CZ-1, WHEEL. A PHYS user record
keeps its slot and loads as a stand-in at the end of the VA's pool (`PHYS retired: stand-in`); a part parked on PHYS
in the settings or the session comes up on its default (FM6 TINE EP, VA DEEP SUB); loops hold no sound; backups
restore. **It freed 9348 B of flash and 12 008 B of POOL** (94.9 -> 91.4 %: the engine-state union is FM6's size
now), RAM +12 B. docs/INTEGRATION.md "0.18: PHYS retired".

The second half of the request, "redistribute the preset slots so you can have more presets for FM6, VA, FM-Tone,
and CZ-1, don't add more preset slots to Wheel, 6 is plenty", **was not built: there is nothing per engine to
redistribute.** How sounds are organised:

| engine | factory presets (ROM, `engine_t.presets`) | pool positions with INIT | the user's |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FM6 | 25 (`eng_fm6.c FM6_PRESETS`: F1..F24 + PIANO) | 26 | any of the 32 shared slots |
| FM TONE | 16 (`eng_quad.c QUAD_PRESETS`) | 17 | any of the 32 |
| VA | 25 (`eng_va.c VA_PRESETS`) | 26 | any of the 32 |
| CZ-1 | 64 (Casio's, `build/gen/melodee_cz1.h`; INIT TONE is the pool's INIT) | 65 | any of the 32 |
| WHEEL | 5 (`eng_wheel.c WHEEL_PRESETS`) | 6 (the "6" of the request) | any of the 32 |

The factory counts are tables in the firmware's flash, not slots: PHYS's nine free nothing another engine could
use. What the user saves goes into **32 user slots shared by all engines** (`UP_SLOTS`, `core.h`; `upreset.c`: two
banks of 16 records of 192 B, A/B at 0xDC000..0xDFFFF, mirrored in RAM, 2 x 3080 B). A slot holds any engine's
sound: either over a factory preset (it replaces that place in the pool) or added after them, so each of the four
engines can already grow by up to 32 and WHEEL by as many. The deep patch of slot k is entry k of its engine's
store, each mirrored in POOL: `va_store.c` (32 x 110 B, one object, 0x97000 / 0x98000), `fm6_ustore.c` (2 x 16 x
128 B, 0x99000..0x9C000), `cz_ustore.c` (2 x 16 x 144 B, 0x9D000 / 0x9E000 and 0xA0000 / 0xA1000), `quad_store.c`
(32 x 80 B, 0xB2000 / 0xB3000); WHEEL (and the retired engines) have none. Besides the slots: FM6's patch bank
B1..B32 (`fm6_bank.c`, backup id 8) and the CZ-1's eight banks of 16 tones (`cz_bank.c`, ids 14..21). Backups carry
the objects whole (ids 6 / 7 the banks, 9, 10 / 11, 12 / 13, 22 the stores); `web/fm1sounds.js`, `fm1manager.js`
and `tools/fm1_install.py` hard-code 32 (`SOUND_SLOTS`) and these ids. What limits the count: an object's payload
(3840 B: the VA's and FM TONE's stores are full at 32, FM6's and the CZ-1's are two objects already), the `used`
masks (32 bits, 16 a half), the mirrors' POOL and RAM, the 18 free sectors 0xB6000..0xC7FFF; not the index widths
(a pool position is 8 bits in the settings, a slot 8 bits in the session; loops name no sound) and not the preset
bar (a stripe per preset up to 32, scaled above: `cu_pool_lit`, unchanged here; the CZ-1's 64 already use it).
The user's unit (`fm1-backups/fm1-backup-now.json`, 2026-10-10, 0.17): 10 of 32 slots used (5 FM TONE, 4 FM6, 1
GRAIN record), 22 free.

Options, for the user to choose:
1. **Nothing** (today): 22 slots free on the user's unit, each open to any of the four engines; deleting a retired
   engine's stand-in frees its slot. No cost.
2. **16 more shared slots (U33..U48).** One more object each for the bank, the VA, FM6, the CZ-1 and FM TONE: five
   A/B pairs = 10 of the 18 free sectors; new backup ids (23..27); the mirrors cost 7456 B of POOL and 3080 B of RAM
   (or 10.5 KB of POOL), about what PHYS freed; `--sounds`, the installer page and the device manager learn 48. No
   migration: the old objects keep their format and place, so 0.14 .. 0.17 sounds stay where they are and an old
   backup restores; a backup of the new build restores on 0.17 for U01..U32 (the tools write what the firmware lists:
   the new ids are skipped and stay in the file); after a step back to 0.17, U33..U48 stay in flash unseen. "Not for
   WHEEL" could only be a rule of Save as new (a WHEEL sound takes a free slot of U01..U32): nothing stops a restore
   or an import placing one. Risk: all five stores, the backup and three clients change shortly before the release.
3. **32 more (U33..U64)**: eight pairs = 16 of the 18 sectors, about 21 KB of mirrors (14.9 KB POOL + 6.2 KB RAM):
   more than PHYS freed, and the free flash is spent.
4. **Slots per engine** (a real split: so many for FM6, FM TONE, VA, CZ-1 each, none for WHEEL): new typed stores (a
   record and its patch together, e.g. 16 an engine: eight pairs, 16 sectors, 19.7 KB of mirrors), a second kind of
   user slot in the UI, the tools and the sound files. The largest change; it is what the request's wording asks for.
5. Either of 2 .. 4 **after the mirrors read through XIP** (open item "Memory", 3t): then slots cost flash only.


**0.18 dev, 2026-10-11 (built, not flashed): SEQ is the loop editor's key; Metronome on the PLAY menu's page 2.** The
owner: "The edit sequence is a bit buried, hold Play, then press edit. Most people won't find this. The Seq key is
currently toggling the metronome on and off. I think from Main level you should be able to press the seq key to enter
the edit sequence menu, press it again to exit. Add Metronome to the second param page of the play menu." And, the
same day, on the first design (SEQ in and out, the editor returning to where it was opened from): "With new SEQ
shortcut to enter editor from main, press once -> sequencer, press SEQ a second time -> Play menu, press SEQ a third
time -> Exit. Also from the Sequencer, holding the play key should enter the Play Menu, not return to home."
`cr_ui.c cu_seq_tap`, a cycle of three: on the view (no layer, no Options, no page) SEQ opens the loop editor, the
PLAY menu locked open under it, with `cu_ledit_open`'s refusals (then nothing); in the editor it closes it to the
PLAY menu, however the editor was opened; in the PLAY menu it closes the menu, however that was entered (EDIT stays
the menu's way into the editor). In the editor **LOOP held** closes the editor and keeps the menu (it closed both:
the layer's own button held again), and a knob turned with LOOP still down is the menu's (PRESETS: the slot); a
LOOP tap is play / stop as it was. EDIT: the PLAY menu, as it was. **HOME from the editor is the view** (it stopped
at the PLAY menu; `cr_loop_edit.txt` and `cr_loop_trig.txt` left the editor by HOME to get to the menu and use EDIT
now). In the other layers, the Options and the save dialog a SEQ tap does nothing (it toggled the click there); the
sound editor keeps it as MOD; the METRO layer (SEQ held) keeps its own button's tap, the click on / off. The click's
switch is the **Metronome** cell on the PLAY menu's page 2, KNOB 2 (the gate glyph, as Count-in; page 2 had Level
alone), and, added by us, **GLO held + SEQ** from anywhere but the sound editor, with the message; `cs.metro` is
stored where it was, no format changed. The SEQ LED showed the click; now it blinks with EDIT's in the loop editor,
is lit in the PLAY menu and dark on the view (the three places of its cycle). The editor's bottom line ends `SEQ:
back` (`EDIT/SEQ` does not fit: `foot` holds 47 characters). SEQ's hold: the METRO layer as before, except in the
loop editor, where SEQ held closes it as EDIT held does and a knob turned with it still down does nothing, and SEQ
held + a knob inside the hold time stays the editor's knob: otherwise PRESETS there (the step cursor) would have
reached the chord sound through the METRO layer, the fault of 2026-10-13 by a new way. Tests:
`tools/emu/scripts/cr_seq.txt` (23 states), `cr_loop_row.txt`, `cr_draw_test.c` (the cell, its label whole); the
manual's `loop-editor.png` / `loop-editor-trig.png` are new (the bottom line). Not renamed: the button is still METRO
in the manual's tables and on the panel pictures, though its tap is the editor now: the owner's call. Seen on the
way: the manual's troubleshooting table sent a silent loop to "PLAY menu, KNOB 3" (Level is page 2, KNOB 1 since
0.18): corrected.

**2026-10-13, the loop editor's grid: what a cell shows (0.18 dev, built, not flashed).** The owner, with the editor on
the unit: "there is no white bar on the selected step, so when the step length is 2, you just see a white bar on the
next step which is confusing and makes you think the next step is selected. So we should always put a white bar also
on the current selected step, and that bar should also become smaller when the step length is below 1, it's a nice
visual touch. The white bars should only render to show length of selected step. The color of the steps is good, but
I think the color should morph colors based on velocity. Also there's a black box that shows when chance is less than
100, I think we should use maybe a yellow color that fades in brightness to black (0% chance), and then also extend
this box to cover the full vertical drawing of the step. If a condition is enabled, render the yellow chance bar now
as blue." And: "When selecting a step we should show the voicing number in blue after the name of the chord." Built
(docs/LOOPER.md "The grid's cells" has the table): the bar begins on the selected step (`lm_bar`: 1 .. 3 quarters of
the cell, 4 the whole) and runs over `lm_lane[2]`, now across the loop's end too; the fill is the velocity in 15
levels (plum (96, 40, 128) at the softest, the role red at 96 .. 104, peach (255, 176, 120) at the loudest), mixed
once per palette; the 3 px notch is a 3 x 7 px strip, yellow dimmed by the chance, blue with a condition; the ratchet
got its own mark, 2 / 3 / 4 ticks of 1 x 2 px along the top (chosen over the bottom edge, where the bar is); the
editor's cursor blinks bright / dim instead of on / off (`lm_ed`; Step entry's grid is as it was); the step's fixed
voicing follows the chord in the role blue (a tab in `sub`; nothing on Follow, which is the default, nor on a step
without a chord). Data: `crl_edmap_t` lost `trig` and got `rat[2]` and a byte a step (`cell`: the velocity's level,
the chance, the condition), 32 -> 104 bytes; `cr_screen_t` got `lm_cell[64]`, a fifth lane, `lm_bar`, `lm_ed`, all in
the part hashed apart, so a cursor move between plain steps still redraws only its rows. POOL 314600 -> 314744 B
(91.4 -> 91.5 %), RAM 80532 -> 80612 B, flash +992 B. Frame cost on the emulator (estimates): the average UI frame
+0.3 % (`cr_loop_step.txt`) and +0.6 % (`cr_loop_trig.txt`), the worst +0.1 % and +1.6 %; not counted on the unit.
Tests: `tests/cr_draw_test.c` (the bar's widths, none elsewhere, the strip's colours and height, the velocity's
brightness, the ticks, the dim cursor, the voicing and its fit), `tests/cr_loop_test.c` (the map); the manual's two
editor pictures regenerated. Not tested: the look on the unit's panel (the plum and the dim strips at its gamma and
viewing angle), 3/4 and 6/8 grids beyond the shared code, the MONO palette.

owner: "Let's make the old per effect send parameters also show as 0-100% to match the first send page. I agree."
And: the editor's sends stay bars. Built (docs/FX.md "One read-out, one step"): `cr_ui.c` `cu_send_pct` (per cent of

`cr_fx_global`: 47 -> 53; `test_persist.sh`: the delay send 37 -> 43). The mock-ups' Send cells
